## 2026-11-19 — [V2][BACKUP] STRICT ANDROID ENCRYPTED ENVELOPE WIRE CONTRACT
- **BASELINE:** V2 General, Master Journal, Work Log and Handoff read; branch was docs-only `f23136c8f204ba4b2a3ab34d5eaae81f48b765f1` and latest executable #274 `44511532937` PASS on `266c5629`. Owner was given a single read-only Oracle service/port/public-route/source-hash command, with no restart/deploy and explicit do-not-share-token notice. No actual operator response has yet been received.
- **ROOT CAUSE:** Client local private backup bridge accepted any nonempty string from injected seal callback as "encrypted", and passed arbitrary strings to unseal callback. Thus integration could accidentally upload or process malformed, plaintext-like envelopes, despite backend's stricter format.
- **FIX:** `android_app/src/local_private_backup_bridge.py` now checks exact V2 envelope v1 keys, KDF identifier, 16-byte salt, 12-byte nonce, canonical base64 and bounded ciphertext length before forwarding/restoring. No FastAPI, cryptography, HTTP or Dhan import into the phone. This is format validation only, not AEAD authentication: approved Android crypto callback must still be physically tested. `tests/test_local_private_backup_bridge.py` now tests valid real server-compatible encrypted envelope with injected callback, rejects extra/invalid fields and malformed text before any decrypt callback and retains strict local settings validation. Test imports backend crypto only in the offline Python regression, not in the Android app.
- **VERIFICATION:** Exact candidate Unified CI to be checked; no new route, CI workflow, Oracle host write, APK, V1 edits, Dhan request, or orders. Android native key storage and password UX remain unverified. NEXT check CI, repair if failed, then do the next bounded code-only security integration without claiming deployment.

## 2026-11-19 — CONTINUED SEGMENT: ALIAS-SAFE RELOGIN + ATOMIC LOGOUT READER SCRUB
- **Owner standing rule:** report but immediately continue actual bounded V2-only work, related safe additions/deletions/fixes permitted without repeatedly asking. Preserve General/Master/Work/Handoff trace; no V1, Oracle production, APK, orders, broker secrets, unverified live provider or Android native login.
- **Start:** V2 branch HEAD `8aab3952d870ab22efc26ce41d80c3892e0f3079` at the beginning of this continuation. Inspected signed-reader slot, Python Keystore binding, HTTPS reader, actual test module, refresh intent and active combined workflow. Historical source CI #199 421/421 at `059ae9ae` is NOT a new post-change PASS.
- **Root cause 1 (alias leak):** recent Keystore relogin preserved old reader until a new signed reader is accepted. However a malformed/injected login returning the exact `self._reader` object could accidentally be accepted as a new session OR, when an origin mismatch/cancellation was handled, invalidate that same good previous reader. Fix [`29a9958`](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/29a9958a23bb8e4705f6d59582c62160d382e833) [skip ci], `app_device_reader_slot.py`: under the same slot lock, reject identical reader alias before any incoming-reader invalidate, then check cancellation generation, approved HTTPS origin and active state before adoption. Test [`3a09726`](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/3a097268e65767be564fbc3ddfb100c2fead66d2): same-reader relogin and mismatched-origin alias both rejected while old session remains active.
- **Root cause 2 (clear gap):** `clear()` used to remove the reader from the slot and release slot ownership lock, then call `reader.invalidate()` later. A concurrent HTTPS response could commit to its read-only cache between these actions. Direct `_begin_sign_in` old-reader removal also invalidated outside the slot lock. Fix [`df77534`](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/df77534469a5fe2254b6535b8243fa96beacbeb1) [skip ci]: perform local bearer invalidation before releasing the slot ownership lock in both clear and direct replacement; Keystore `preserve_previous=True` deliberately keeps usable signed reader until successful replacement. This does NOT hold a slot lock during HTTP/network calls. Test [`5bb42b5`](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/commit/5bb42b57c1f3d4c9a33c661f2bdfebbb7248b91c): real in-memory `V2ReadonlyHTTPSReader`, spy `invalidate()` asserts ownership lock held and bearer inactive on clear/direct replacement; retained-reader Keystore preflight has no early invalidate.
- **Next segment audited, no code change needed:** read `refresh_intent.py` and `smart_data.py`; `make_refresh_intent(...,manual=True,confirmed_session=False)` intentionally permits after-close manual intent generation independently of market-session confirmation, while real HTTPS feed dispatch still requires `V2ReadonlyHTTPSReader.session_active`. Do not change this correct rule or mislabel planning as live data.
- **Actual bounded static inspection:** re-fetched modified source, tests, refresh intent and workflow. Nine structural invariants checked YES 9/9: alias before origin invalidation under slot lock, clear and direct-replacement invalidate while owning lock, Keystore preservation, negative tests present, manual after-hours policy unchanged, combined Python unittest workflow intact. **9/9 is STRUCTURAL SOURCE REVIEW ONLY**, not Python regression nor Android runtime or push CI PASS.

## V2 SELECTED READ-ONLY HTTP 429 COOL DOWN — 2026-11-19
- [CI #195](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/actions/runs/44467420442) VERIFIED SUCCESS 417/417, security findings 0, UI source wiring 9/9. Session invalidate and validated read-only cache delivery share a short lock, so no quote can be committed after local logout; the lock is never held across network I/O.
- Continued Smart Data read-only HTTP pacing: a server HTTP 429 previously raised an error but allowed immediate calls again, potentially increasing provider/server load. Device reader now sets a bounded local 30-second monotonic backoff on either HTTPError 429 or direct response.status=429. During backoff, no index or equity HTTP request opens; local bearer remains available (429 is NOT a revocation), last-good cache unchanged, and after expiry normal authorized GET can proceed. - Audited `DeviceReaderSlot.sign_in`: old logic called `clear()` then captured `_generation` under a second lock acquisition, permitting an intervening concurrent login/logout to change generation between those actions. ## V2-OWNER-STANDING-WORKFLOW-20261118 — owner continuity / permissions policy
- **Owner's intention:** Complete V2 end-to-end as autonomously as feasible, using already-approved design; avoid repeated micro-permission prompts. User may stop/change scope anytime.

No need to seek recurring permission simply to append journals.
- **NOT blanket permission:** No unreviewed production Oracle service overwrite/restart, credential extraction/rotation, broker login bypass, third-party user access invitations, provider subscriptions or charges, real-money orders, deletion of historical app data, legacy service shutdown or forced switch, or external user/device enrollment. ## V2-FT2-ISOLATED-INDEX-EQUITY-REVISION-INBOX-20261118 — app-only two-lane authenticated-feed contract preparation
- **Prior gate:** FT1 signed P256 Master+3 delegated cross-worker SHA `6cc7d6e48aa6cd394046f90a3ca2ed3fb0561289` exact CI #40 `44316203468` SUCCESS. Last branch commit `b3e6911ad3682bcdb527b4c57ed7a403f97fa26e` docs-only.
- **Source-grounded risk:** existing `DeviceQuoteView.apply` had one revision counter for BOTH separately fetched `/v2-pilot/quotes` and `/v2-pilot/indices`. Two endpoints can legitimately return different monotonically increasing revisions. If an index payload revision=200 arrives first, a valid newer EQUITY feed payload revision=1 would be rejected as old. This is a contract-integration mismatch; no live production symptom claimed.
- **Scoped Flet-only fix:** `pilot_v2/android_app/src/feed_inbox.py` wraps two existing validated `DeviceQuoteView` instances. Explicit caller-supplied `quotes` allows only selected equities, `indices` only the three separate indexes, with separate monotone clocks and shared WAIT/HIST presentation. App-owned selections never written to server. `main.py` consumes inbox instead of one mixed clock. No HTTP listener/client, no actual bearer/session, no synchronous polling, and no API/Oracle module changed.
- **New focused tests:** `pilot_v2/tests/test_ft2_separate_feed_lanes.py` checks out-of-order independently valid lanes, source-only filtering, partial empty retention, invalid source/foreign feed fail-closed, deletion privacy and no invented LIVE. Prior legacy single-snapshot tests remain unchanged. Full isolated exact-SHA GitHub CI **PENDING** at commit authoring.
- **Deferred for final acceptance:** Real authenticated HTTPS fetch/client dispatch, native master pairing, app lifecycle persistence and Flet/Android runtime need verification. This source prep does not certify mobile data or Oracle production. No Dhan/token modification, server/backup/restart, orders or APK. NEXT exact CI, then owner-ordered FT2 remaining app-only client-contract tests followed by FT3 repair.
